# Acceptance Criteria: Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

<!-- HVR_REFERENCE: .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-with-human-voice/references/hvr-rules.md -->

> This document decides whether the packet may close. A packet is closeable when
> every row below is `Met`, `Waived` or `Superseded`. A `Waived` or `Superseded`
> row MUST name an ADR that exists in `decision-record.md`.

---

**Packet:** system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

**Status:** In Progress

### Status values

| Value | Meaning |
|-------|---------|
| `Met` | Verified. The Verification cell names evidence that was actually observed. |
| `Unmet` | Not yet satisfied. Blocks closure. |
| `Waived` | Deliberately not pursued. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |
| `Superseded` | Replaced by a different criterion or decision. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |

### Waiver cell

Write `-` when the row is `Met` or `Unmet`. Write `ADR-NNN` when the row is
`Waived` or `Superseded`, naming a decision record that exists in
`decision-record.md`. A waiver naming an ADR that is not there fails validation:
the point of a waiver is that someone recorded the reasoning, so an unbacked
waiver is treated as an unmet criterion rather than as a pass.
<!-- /ANCHOR:criteria -->

**Closeable:** No

Twelve of the fifteen rows are Met. AC-010 stays Unmet because the parent phase map holds eight Complete rows and phase 9 reads In Progress, AC-011 stays Unmet because `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt` holds six PASS lines and the C5 FAIL line, and AC-015 stays Unmet because the phase cannot read Complete while criterion 5 is FAIL. The packet closes only after the follow-up benchmark rerun passes criterion 5.

# Tasks: Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

<!-- ANCHOR:notation -->
## Task Notation

| Prefix | Meaning |
|--------|---------|
| `[ ]` | Pending |
| `[x]` | Completed |
| `[P]` | Parallelizable |
| `[B]` | Blocked |

**Task Format**: `T### [P?] Description (file path)`, then `Verify:` with the command whose output proves the task.

Shell variables used below, set once by the conductor:
- `WORKTREE`: the build worktree path recorded in `../goal.md` section 4.
- `PKT`: `$WORKTREE/specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring`
- `PHASE`: `$PKT/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout`
- `SC`: `$PHASE/scratch`
- `S`: `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit`
- `P1`: `$PKT/001-template-drift-census-and-baseline`
- `BL`: `$P1/scratch/baseline-swe-l3`, the Level 3 cli-devin swe-2-max baseline.
- `P3`: `$PKT/003-versioned-placeholder-gate`
- `START`: the commit hash in `$SC/baseline/start-commit.txt`, recorded by T003.

Every command runs from `$WORKTREE` unless the task says otherwise. Numbers printed in a Verify line that depend on the run are written as a rule, not as a fixed value. The baseline side is read from `$BL`: `baseline.json` for the three runs, `brief-1.txt` for the frozen brief, `hints-l3.txt` for the bare hint list and `anchors-l3.txt` for the required anchors.

## Phase 1: Setup

- [x] T001 Check the tools and the configured model (no file). Verify: `devin auth status` prints `Logged in`, and `for t in jq rg awk node python3 git perl sqlite3 devin; do command -v "$t" || echo "MISSING $t"; done` prints nine paths and no `MISSING` line.

- [x] T002 Check that every prior phase and the write-recipe child are closed (no file). Run the block below. Verify: the first line reads `8 RESULT: PASSED`, the second reads `RESULT: PASSED`, then `BASELINE_OK`, `CENSUS_OK`, `DRIFT_GUARD_OK`, `CARD_GUARD_OK`, `NESTED_OK` and a final `RESULT: PASSED`. When one line is missing or a child fails, write the missing path or the failing child in `goal.md` section 4 and stop the phase. This phase does not edit another phase folder.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  for d in $PKT/00[1-8]-*/; do bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$d" --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1; done | sort | uniq -c | sed 's/^ *//'
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh specs/system-speckit/041-write-recipe-fixes/006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1
  test -s "$BL/baseline.json" && jq -e '.runs | length == 3' "$BL/baseline.json" > /dev/null && test -s "$BL/brief-1.txt" && test -s "$BL/hints-l3.txt" && test -s "$BL/anchors-l3.txt" && echo BASELINE_OK
  test -s "$P3/scratch/census/folders.txt" && test -s "$P3/scratch/census/b-before.tsv" && test -s "$P3/scratch/census/shard-0.txt" && echo CENSUS_OK
  test -f "$S/runtime/cli/spec/check-example-drift.sh" && echo DRIFT_GUARD_OK
  test -f "$S/runtime/cli/spec/check-card-sync.sh" && echo CARD_GUARD_OK
  test -s "$S/runtime/cli/dist/spec-folder/nested-changelog.js" && echo NESTED_OK
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PKT" --recursive --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1
  ```

- [x] T003 Record the start state (`goal.md`, `scratch/baseline/status-start.txt`, `scratch/baseline/suite-final.txt`). Run the block below, then write the hash into `goal.md` section 4 as a row `| Phase start commit | Done | <the 40 character hash> |`. Verify: `grep -cE 'Phase start commit \| Done \| [0-9a-f]{40}' "$PHASE/goal.md"` prints `1`, `tail -1 "$SC/baseline/suite-final.txt"` prints `exit=0`, and `wc -l < "$SC/baseline/status-start.txt"` prints a number. A suite exit other than 0 stops the phase with the failing summary lines in `goal.md` section 4.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  mkdir -p "$SC/baseline" "$SC/runs" "$SC/briefs" "$SC/review" "$SC/closeout" "$SC/index" "$SC/tools" "$SC/test"
  git rev-parse HEAD | tee "$SC/baseline/start-commit.txt"
  git status --porcelain > "$SC/baseline/status-start.txt"
  npm --prefix "$WORKTREE/$S" test > "$SC/baseline/suite-final.txt" 2>&1; echo "exit=$?" >> "$SC/baseline/suite-final.txt"
  ```

## Phase 2: Implementation

- [x] T004 Build the final vocabulary and anchor list (`scratch/baseline/hints-v2.3.txt`, `scratch/baseline/anchors-v2.3.txt`). Run the block below. It scaffolds one Level 3 packet, the level the runs use, extracts the distinct value tokens and the anchor count of each of the four documents, then deletes the throwaway track. Both files record the final templates. M3 in T006 still measures against `$BL/anchors-l3.txt`, so both sides share one anchor definition. Verify: `wc -l < "$SC/baseline/hints-v2.3.txt"` prints a number above 50, `cat "$SC/baseline/anchors-v2.3.txt"` prints four lines, and `test ! -e specs/zz-bench-hints && echo TRACK_GONE` prints `TRACK_GONE`. Record both counts in `goal.md` section 4.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 3 --track zz-bench-hints --short-name recommend-level-quiet-flag "Add a quiet flag to recommend-level.sh"
  P=specs/zz-bench-hints/001-recommend-level-quiet-flag
  rg -o --no-filename '\[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: [^]]*\]' $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md $P/acceptance-criteria.md | sort -u > "$SC/baseline/hints-v2.3.txt"
  for f in spec plan tasks acceptance-criteria; do echo "$f $(rg -c '<!-- ANCHOR:' $P/$f.md)"; done > "$SC/baseline/anchors-v2.3.txt"
  rm -r specs/zz-bench-hints
  ```

- [x] T005 Run the final baseline three times, serially (`scratch/runs/T005-1.txt` to `T005-3.txt`, `scratch/baseline/runs.jsonl`). For each run `N` in 1, 2, 3, in order:
  1. Save `git status --porcelain` to `$SC/baseline/status-before-$N.txt`.
  2. Save `date +%s` to `$SC/runs/T005-$N.t0` before the dispatch.
  3. Run the dispatch block below. It copies the frozen brief and runs it on cli-devin swe-2-max, the benchmark subject. Run nothing else in the worktree while the run is live, because any write there counts as the subject's out-of-scope write.
  4. Run the audit block below. Each of its three lines must end in `0`. A higher count means the subject read baseline evidence, an earlier run or a conductor file: record the run as contaminated in `goal.md` section 4, keep it in `runs.jsonl`, and stop the phase for the operator.
  5. Run T006 for that run before starting the next one.

  Verify: `for n in 1 2 3; do test -s "$SC/runs/T005-$n.txt" && test -s "$SC/runs/T005-$n.t0" && test -s "$SC/runs/T005-$n.t1" && test -s "$SC/baseline/status-before-$n.txt" && echo "run-$n recorded"; done` prints `run-1 recorded`, `run-2 recorded` and `run-3 recorded`, and the frozen-text check prints `TASK_FROZEN`.

  Dispatch block for run `N`. The brief is the Level 3 baseline brief with no word changed:

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  cp "$BL/brief-1.txt" "$SC/briefs/T005-$N.txt"
  AI_SESSION_CHILD=1 SYSTEM_SPEC_GATE_ENFORCE=0 devin -p --model swe-2-max --permission-mode dangerous -- "$(cat "$SC/briefs/T005-$N.txt")" </dev/null > "$SC/runs/T005-$N.txt" 2> "$SC/runs/T005-$N.err"
  date +%s > "$SC/runs/T005-$N.t1"
  ```

  Audit block for run `N`. It finds the run's Devin session by working directory and start time and counts tool calls that name a forbidden path:

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  DB=~/.local/share/devin/cli/sessions.db
  SID=$(sqlite3 -readonly "$DB" "select id from sessions where working_directory='$WORKTREE' and created_at >= $(cat "$SC/runs/T005-$N.t0") order by created_at limit 1;")
  echo "$SID" > "$SC/runs/T005-$N.session"
  sqlite3 -readonly "$DB" "select tool_call_json from tool_call_state where session_id='$SID';" > "$SC/runs/T005-$N.calls"
  for p in baseline-swe 009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch /private/tmp; do echo "$p $(grep -o "$p" "$SC/runs/T005-$N.calls" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')"; done
  ```

  Frozen-text check, run after the three briefs exist:

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  CHANGED=0; for n in 1 2 3; do cmp -s "$BL/brief-1.txt" "$SC/briefs/T005-$n.txt" || { echo "BRIEF_CHANGED $n"; CHANGED=1; }; done; [ "$CHANGED" = 0 ] && echo TASK_FROZEN
  ```

- [x] T006 Measure each final run, then delete its track (part of `scratch/baseline/baseline.json`). Run the block below after run `N` stops, with `N` set to the run number, before the next dispatch. Verify: after the third run, `jq '.runs | length' "$SC/baseline/baseline.json"` prints `3`, `jq '.runs[] | keys | length' "$SC/baseline/baseline.json"` prints `6` three times, and `test ! -e specs/zz-bench-authoring && echo TRACK_GONE` prints `TRACK_GONE`.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  P=specs/zz-bench-authoring/001-recommend-level-quiet-flag
  HL3="$BL/hints-l3.txt"
  AL3="$BL/anchors-l3.txt"
  T0=$(cat "$SC/runs/T005-$N.t0")
  M1=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep -c 'RESULT: PASSED')
  M2=$( { grep -F -o -f "$HL3" $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md $P/acceptance-criteria.md 2>/dev/null; rg -o --no-filename '\[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: [^]]*\]' $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md $P/acceptance-criteria.md 2>/dev/null; } | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  M3=0; while read f n; do have=$(rg -c '<!-- ANCHOR:' $P/$f.md 2>/dev/null || echo 0); [ "$have" -lt "$n" ] && M3=$((M3 + n - have)); done < "$AL3"
  git status --porcelain > "$SC/baseline/status-after-$N.txt"
  M4=$(diff "$SC/baseline/status-before-$N.txt" "$SC/baseline/status-after-$N.txt" | grep '^>' | grep -v 'zz-bench-authoring' | grep -v '054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  M5=$(( $(date +%s) - T0 ))
  echo "{\"run\":$N,\"strict_passed\":$M1,\"leftover_hints\":$M2,\"missing_anchors\":$M3,\"out_of_scope_writes\":$M4,\"seconds\":$M5}" >> "$SC/baseline/runs.jsonl"
  jq -s '{runs: .}' "$SC/baseline/runs.jsonl" > "$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
  rm -r specs/zz-bench-authoring
  ```

- [x] T007 Write the delta for the five metrics (`scratch/baseline/delta.json`, `scratch/baseline/delta.md`). Run the block below. Verify: `jq '.metrics | length' "$SC/baseline/delta.json"` prints `5`, `grep -cE '^\| (strict_passed|leftover_hints|missing_anchors|out_of_scope_writes|seconds) \|' "$SC/baseline/delta.md"` prints `5`, and the last command prints `true`. When it prints `false`, write `C5 FAILED: a final run failed strict validation or is worse than the baseline's worst on leftover_hints, missing_anchors or out_of_scope_writes` in `goal.md` section 4, run T008 only, then write `STOP` in `goal.md` section 4 and stop the closeout. Do not tick the parent criterion and do not publish a changelog.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  B="$BL/baseline.json"
  F="$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
  jq -n --slurpfile b "$B" --slurpfile f "$F" '
    def stat($runs; $k):
      {min: ($runs | map(.[$k]) | min),
       max: ($runs | map(.[$k]) | max),
       mean: (($runs | map(.[$k]) | add) / ($runs | length))};
    ["strict_passed","leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes","seconds"] as $keys
    | {metrics: ($keys | map(. as $k | {metric: $k, baseline: stat($b[0].runs; $k), final: stat($f[0].runs; $k)}))}
    | .metrics |= map(. + {delta_mean: (.final.mean - .baseline.mean)})
  ' > "$SC/baseline/delta.json"
  jq -r '
    def one: (.*10 | round) / 10;
    "# Authoring delta\n",
    "- Baseline: three Level 3 runs of the frozen brief on cli-devin swe-2-max, recorded in `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/baseline.json`.",
    "- Final: three runs of the same task, recorded in `scratch/baseline/baseline.json`.",
    "- Each cell is the minimum, the maximum and the mean over three runs.",
    "- Three runs cannot support a significance claim. The table reports a range, not an effect size.\n",
    "| Metric | Baseline min | Baseline max | Baseline mean | Final min | Final max | Final mean | Delta of means |",
    "|--------|--------------|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|",
    (.metrics[] | "| \(.metric) | \(.baseline.min) | \(.baseline.max) | \(.baseline.mean | one) | \(.final.min) | \(.final.max) | \(.final.mean | one) | \(.delta_mean | one) |")
  ' "$SC/baseline/delta.json" > "$SC/baseline/delta.md"
  jq -e 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max))' "$SC/baseline/delta.json"
  ```

- [x] T008 Write the benchmark report and its index row (`$S/benchmark/reports/$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline/`, `$S/benchmark/reports/README.md`). Read `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/spec.md`, `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/plan.md` and `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/implementation-summary.md` for the report shape phase 8 accepted, and read `.skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-benchmark/SKILL.md` sections 3 and 5 for the required package shape and the report contract, before composing the report. Run the block below, which writes `results.csv`, `README.md` and `source.md` from `delta.json` and inserts one index row. Verify: `awk 'NR>1' "$REPORT/results.csv" | wc -l` prints `6`, `grep -c '^## [0-9]' "$REPORT/README.md"` prints `10`, `grep -c 'model-eval--authoring-baseline' "$S/benchmark/reports/README.md"` prints `1`, and the script prints `NAME_OK`.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  DATE=$(date +%F)
  NAME="$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline"
  REPORT="$S/benchmark/reports/$NAME"
  DELTA="$SC/baseline/delta.json"
  B="$BL/baseline.json"
  F="$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
  echo "$NAME" | grep -qE '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}--[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*--[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$' && echo NAME_OK
  mkdir -p "$REPORT"
  {
    echo 'side,run,strict_passed,leftover_hints,missing_anchors,out_of_scope_writes,seconds'
    jq -r '.runs[] | "baseline,\(.run),\(.strict_passed),\(.leftover_hints),\(.missing_anchors),\(.out_of_scope_writes),\(.seconds)"' "$B"
    jq -r '.runs[] | "final,\(.run),\(.strict_passed),\(.leftover_hints),\(.missing_anchors),\(.out_of_scope_writes),\(.seconds)"' "$F"
  } > "$REPORT/results.csv"
  val() { jq -r --arg m "$1" --arg s "$2" --arg k "$3" '.metrics[] | select(.metric==$m) | .[$s][$k]' "$DELTA"; }
  L_B_MIN=$(val leftover_hints baseline min); L_B_MAX=$(val leftover_hints baseline max)
  L_F_MIN=$(val leftover_hints final min);   L_F_MAX=$(val leftover_hints final max)
  VERDICT=$(jq -r 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max)) | if . then "PASS" else "FAIL" end' "$DELTA")
  {
    printf '# %s\n\n' "$NAME"
    printf '> system-spec-kit · model-eval · authoring-baseline · cli-devin swe-2-max\n\n---\n\n'
    printf '## 1. HEADLINE / OVERVIEW\n\n'
    printf '**Verdict: %s**\n\n' "$VERDICT"
    printf 'Three baseline runs and three final runs of one frozen Level 3 authoring brief. Leftover hints run from %s to %s on the baseline side and from %s to %s on the final side. The verdict is PASS when every final run passes strict validation and none is worse than the worst baseline run on leftover hints, missing anchors or out-of-scope writes.\n\n' "$L_B_MIN" "$L_B_MAX" "$L_F_MIN" "$L_F_MAX"
    printf '## 2. AGGREGATE RESULTS\n\n'
    echo '| Metric | Baseline min | Baseline max | Baseline mean | Final min | Final max | Final mean | Delta of means |'
    echo '|--------|--------------|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|'
    jq -r 'def one: (.*10 | round) / 10; .metrics[] | "| \(.metric) | \(.baseline.min) | \(.baseline.max) | \(.baseline.mean | one) | \(.final.min) | \(.final.max) | \(.final.mean | one) | \(.delta_mean | one) |"' "$DELTA"
    printf '\n## 3. METHODOLOGY\n\n'
    cat <<'MD' | sed 's/^  //'
  Fixture: one Level 3 packet for a `--quiet` flag on `recommend-level.sh`. The brief is frozen and copied from the phase 1 baseline with no word changed. Each side is three serial runs by cli-devin `swe-2-max`, and each run's Devin session is audited for reads of baseline evidence. The measured documents are `spec.md`, `plan.md`, `tasks.md` and `acceptance-criteria.md`. The throwaway track is deleted after each measurement.

  ## 4. PER-CANDIDATE PROFILES

  | Side | Tree | What the worker saw |
  |---|---|---|
  | baseline | Before the packet changes | v2.2 templates, bare bracketed hints, the hand-written examples |
  | final | After phases 1 to 8 | v2.3 value tokens, section fill guidance, the authoring card, the guards |

  ## 5. PROCESS NOTES

  The baseline side ran in phase 1 on templates identical to the start commit and was not rerun. The final side ran after every phase closed, so the worker saw the tree the packet ships. A run that left its track behind was restored before the next dispatch.

  ## 6. FINDINGS

  The load-bearing numbers are the worst final run on leftover hints, missing anchors and out-of-scope writes, read against the worst baseline run. The baseline already reached 0 hints in one run, so the packet claims no regression rather than a lower minimum. A wide final range means the templates help some runs and not others.

  ## 7. CAVEATS

  Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim. The fixture is one small feature and four documents. The model is one model at one effort. A template change and a card change landed together, so this report cannot separate their effects.

  ## 8. RECOMMENDATIONS

  - **Apply now.** Keep the v2.3 token gate and the card. Both are measured on the same task.
  - **Validate first.** Rerun this report after the next template change with a new date and the same variant.
  - **Future.** Add a second feature shape if a future packet needs to know whether the gain holds outside one small feature.

  ## 9. REPRODUCIBILITY

MD
    printf '%s\n' '```bash' \
      'bash .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 3 --track zz-bench-hints --short-name recommend-level-quiet-flag "Add a quiet flag to recommend-level.sh"' \

    printf '| Delta | `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/baseline/delta.json` |\n'
    printf '| Run briefs | `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/briefs/T005-*.txt` |\n'
    printf '| Model | cli-devin `swe-2-max` |\n\n'
    printf '## 2. BOUNDARY\n\n'
    printf 'The corpus is an input and is never rewritten by a run. This folder holds outputs only, and a run whose result changes gets a new folder rather than overwriting this one.\n\n'
    printf 'Last updated %s.\n' "$DATE"
  } > "$REPORT/source.md"
  ROW="| $DATE | [\`$NAME/\`](./$NAME/) | cli-devin swe-2-max, frozen Level 3 authoring brief | 3 baseline and 3 final runs, 5 metrics | **$VERDICT** | \`054-weak-model-spec-authoring\` |"
  ROW="$ROW" perl -pi -e 'if (/^## 2\. RUN INDEX/) { $in = 1 } if ($in && /^\|---\|/ && !$done) { $_ .= "$ENV{ROW}\n"; $done = 1 }' "$S/benchmark/reports/README.md"
  echo INDEX_ROW_ADDED
  python3 .skilled/skills/sk-doc/shared/scripts/validate_document.py "$REPORT/README.md" | tail -2

  # v2.9.0.0, Templates a Weak Model Can Follow

  A weak model can now see what a template still needs, because every hint in the five core templates is a value token that fails validation once the packet leaves Draft. The four authoring intents also load a short card rather than the long guide, and the examples no longer teach the defects the old text carried.

  > Spec folder: `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring` (Level 2)

  &nbsp;

  ## What's New at a Glance

  - **An unfilled hint can no longer pass as finished.** A doc stamped v2.3 warns while the spec is Draft and fails strict validation at any other status.
  - **Every core section says how to fill it.** Each section carries a short instruction and a good and weak example.
  - **Authoring starts on one page.** The router loads a short card for the four authoring intents.
  - **The examples stop teaching the old defects.** Their descriptions, stale checklist lines and anchors now match the templates.
  - **Commands and agents name the new gate and the card.** A speckit or doctor run describes the same authoring path a worker follows.
  - **The playbook and catalog cover the new behavior.** A focused run report records each scenario result.
  - **The result is measured, not asserted.** Three runs of one frozen authoring task on each side give a range for five metrics.

  &nbsp;

  ## Upgrade

  No migration required. Existing packets keep their verdict, because the token gate applies only to docs stamped v2.3. Scaffold a new packet to get the new templates and the authoring card.

  ## How It Was Delivered

  The conductor ran three serial cli-devin swe-2-max dispatches of the frozen Level 3 brief, audited each run's Devin session, measured each run with the five commands in `tasks.md` T006, wrote every number from `delta.json`, and proved each parent criterion before ticking its box.   - This phase made one local commit on the worktree branch. Nothing was pushed.

  - No generated artifact needs a hand edit before the commit.

  ## Key Decisions

  | Decision | Why |
  |----------|-----|
  | Count bare hints plus value tokens for M2 | The two patterns are disjoint, so each unfilled instruction counts once on both sides |
  | Name the report `model-eval--authoring-baseline` | The run measures a model on the authoring task, and the feature name lets a later model reuse it |
  | Prove parent criterion 3 on the frozen folder list | A live walk would compare different populations |
  <!-- /ANCHOR:decisions -->

  ---

  <!-- ANCHOR:verification -->
  ## Verification

  | Check | Result |
  |-------|--------|
  | Recursive strict validation on the packet | `RESULT: PASSED` |
  | Strict validation on this phase folder | `RESULT: PASSED` |
  | `check-goal.cjs` on the parent and nine children | `RESULT: PASSED (5/5 checks)` ten times |
  | Trigger index check | exit 0 |
  | Track root dry run | `system-speckit: up to date`, exit 0 |
  | Report rows | 6 |
  | Seven parent criteria | 7 PASS lines |
  | Benchmark report markdown | `Total issues: 0` |
  <!-- /ANCHOR:verification -->

  ---

  <!-- ANCHOR:limitations -->
  ## Known Limitations

  1. **Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim.** The report gives a range and says so.
  2. **The fixture is one small feature and four documents.** A different feature shape or a Level 1 packet is not measured.
  3. **The template gate and the authoring card landed together.** This report cannot separate their effects.
  <!-- /ANCHOR:limitations -->

  ---
  ```

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  DATE=$(date +%F)
  REPORT_NAME="$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline"
  L_B_MIN=$(jq -r '.metrics[] | select(.metric=="leftover_hints") | .baseline.min' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
  L_F_MIN=$(jq -r '.metrics[] | select(.metric=="leftover_hints") | .final.min' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
  perl -pi -e 's/\| Unmet \| - \|/| Met | - |/g' "$PHASE/acceptance-criteria.md"
  perl -pi -e 's/^\*\*Closeable:\*\* No$/**Closeable:** Yes/' "$PHASE/acceptance-criteria.md"
  perl -pi -e 's/^\*\*Status:\*\* Draft$/**Status:** Complete/' "$PHASE/acceptance-criteria.md"
  perl -0pi -e 's/The phase has not started\. This statement is rewritten when the phase closes\./The packet closed on the seven parent proofs in `scratch\/closeout\/criteria.txt` and the fifteen acceptance rows in this file./' "$PHASE/acceptance-criteria.md"
  perl -pi -e "s/__DATE__/$DATE/g; s/__L_B_MIN__/$L_B_MIN/g; s/__L_F_MIN__/$L_F_MIN/g; s/__REPORT_NAME__/$REPORT_NAME/g" "$PHASE/implementation-summary.md"
  perl -pi -e 's/\| \*\*Status\*\* \| Draft \|/| **Status** | Complete |/' "$PHASE/spec.md"
  ```

## Phase 3: Verification

- [x] T015 Prove each of the seven criteria in `../goal.md` section 3 (`scratch/closeout/criteria.txt`). Write the script below and run it. Each block prints one line, `C<n> PASS <evidence>` or `C<n> FAIL <reason>`. Verify: `grep -c '^C[1-7] PASS ' "$SC/closeout/criteria.txt"` prints `7` and `rg -c '^\| Parent criteria \| Done \| 7 PASS lines' "$PKT/goal.md"` prints `1`. When a line reads FAIL, write it in `goal.md` section 4, leave the matching parent box unticked in T017 and do not claim the packet is closeable. **Partly met (record reconcile on 2026-10-25).** The verify line asks for seven PASS lines, but criteria.txt holds six PASS lines and the C5 FAIL line, which is the contingency this task describes. The task text only asks to record a FAIL and leave its parent box unticked, which was done.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  C="$SC/closeout/criteria.txt"
  : > "$C"

  # C1: recursive strict on the packet.
  OUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PKT" --recursive --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1)
  if [ "$OUT" = "RESULT: PASSED" ]; then echo "C1 PASS $OUT" >> "$C"; else echo "C1 FAIL $OUT" >> "$C"; fi

  # C2: the token gate on a fresh v2.3 packet and on an old stamp.
  rm -rf specs/zz-bench-closeout
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 1 --track zz-bench-closeout --short-name gate "Gate" > "$SC/closeout/c2-create.txt" 2>&1
  P=specs/zz-bench-closeout/001-gate
  D=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1)
  perl -pi -e 's/\| \*\*Status\*\* \| Draft \|/| **Status** | Complete |/' $P/spec.md
  K=$(env SPECKIT_RULES=PLACEHOLDER_FILLED bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep -c 'placeholder(s) found')
  perl -pi -e 's/\| \*\*Status\*\* \| Complete \|/| **Status** | Draft |/' $P/spec.md
  perl -pi -e 's/\| v2\.3 -->/| v2.2 -->/' $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md
  V=$(env SPECKIT_RULES=PLACEHOLDER_FILLED bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep -c 'placeholder(s) found')
  rm -rf specs/zz-bench-closeout
  if [ "$D" = "RESULT: PASSED" ] && [ "$K" -ge 1 ] && [ "$V" -ge 1 ]; then echo "C2 PASS draft=$D complete_errors=$K v22_errors=$V" >> "$C"; else echo "C2 FAIL draft=$D complete_errors=$K v22_errors=$V" >> "$C"; fi

  # C3: the frozen phase 3 census, rerun on the final tree.
  ORCH="$S/runtime/dist/lib/validation/orchestrator.js"
  for i in 0 1 2; do env -u SPECKIT_RULES node "$P3/scratch/tools/strict-census.mjs" "$ORCH" "$P3/scratch/census/shard-$i.txt" > "$SC/closeout/b-final-$i.tsv" 2> "$SC/closeout/b-final-$i.err" & done
  wait
  cat "$SC/closeout/b-final-"[012].tsv | LC_ALL=C sort > "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv"
  cut -f1,2,5 "$P3/scratch/census/b-before.tsv" > "$SC/closeout/b-before.cut"
  cut -f1,2,5 "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv" > "$SC/closeout/b-final.cut"
  DIFFROWS=$(diff "$SC/closeout/b-before.cut" "$SC/closeout/b-final.cut" | grep -c '^[<>]')
  BCOUNT=$(cut -f2 "$P3/scratch/census/b-before.tsv" | sort | uniq -c | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ' ')
  FCOUNT=$(cut -f2 "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv" | sort | uniq -c | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ' ')
  if [ "$DIFFROWS" = "0" ]; then echo "C3 PASS CENSUS_SAME gate B before[$BCOUNT] after[$FCOUNT]" >> "$C"; else echo "C3 FAIL diff_rows=$DIFFROWS before[$BCOUNT] after[$FCOUNT]" >> "$C"; fi

  # C4: the two guards.
  AOUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/check-example-drift.sh 2>&1); AR=$?
  A=$(printf '%s\n' "$AOUT" | tail -1)
  BOUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/check-card-sync.sh 2>&1); BR=$?
  B=$(printf '%s\n' "$BOUT" | tail -1)
  if [ "$A" = "EXAMPLE_DRIFT_OK" ] && [ "$B" = "CARD SYNC OK" ] && [ "$AR" = "0" ] && [ "$BR" = "0" ]; then echo "C4 PASS $A $B" >> "$C"; else echo "C4 FAIL $A rc=$AR $B rc=$BR" >> "$C"; fi

  # C5: the report covers five metrics on two sides of three runs, and no final run regressed against the worst baseline run.
  N=$(jq '.metrics | length' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
  BRUNS=$(jq '.runs | length' "$BL/baseline.json")
  FRUNS=$(jq '.runs | length' "$SC/baseline/baseline.json")
  LOW=$(jq -r '([.metrics[] | select(.metric=="leftover_hints")][0]) | "\(.baseline.max) -> \(.final.max)"' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
  OK=$(jq -r 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max)) | if . then "yes" else "no" end' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
  if [ "$N" = "5" ] && [ "$BRUNS" = "3" ] && [ "$FRUNS" = "3" ] && [ "$OK" = "yes" ]; then echo "C5 PASS metrics=$N runs=$BRUNS+$FRUNS leftover_max=$LOW" >> "$C"; else echo "C5 FAIL metrics=$N runs=$BRUNS+$FRUNS leftover_max=$LOW" >> "$C"; fi

  # C6: every playbook scenario this packet added has a PASS or an escalated FAIL.
  REPORTS="$S/benchmark/reports"
  REPORT=$(for d in "$REPORTS"/*--manual-testing-playbook--*/; do test -s "$d/source.md" && rg -q '054-weak-model-spec-authoring' "$d/source.md" && echo "$d"; done | sort | tail -1)
  ADDED=$({ git diff --name-only "$START" -- "$S/manual-testing-playbook/"; git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$S/manual-testing-playbook/"; } | sort -u | rg '\.md$' | rg -v 'manual-testing-playbook\.md$')
  MISS=0; LINES=""
  if [ -z "$REPORT" ]; then echo "C6 FAIL MISSING-008-REPORT" >> "$C"; elif [ -z "$ADDED" ]; then echo "C6 FAIL NO_SCENARIO_FILES" >> "$C"; else
    for f in $ADDED; do
      SLUG=$(basename "$(dirname "$f")")-$(basename "$f" .md)
      NUM=$(rg -m1 -o '^title: "([0-9]+)' -r '$1' "$f")
      V=$(awk -F, -v id="$SLUG" -v num="$NUM" 'NR==1{for(i=1;i<=NF;i++){if($i=="scenario_id")s=i; if($i=="verdict")v=i}; next} ($s==id || $s==num){print $v}' "$REPORT/results.csv" | head -1)
      if [ "$V" != "PASS" ]; then
        if rg -q 'FAIL' "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md" && { rg -q --fixed-strings "$SLUG" "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md" || rg -q --fixed-strings "$NUM" "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md"; }; then V="FAIL-LOGGED"; else MISS=$((MISS+1)); fi
      fi
      LINES="$LINES $SLUG=$V"
    done
    if [ "$MISS" = "0" ]; then echo "C6 PASS$LINES" >> "$C"; else echo "C6 FAIL missing=$MISS$LINES" >> "$C"; fi
  fi

  # C7: swarm commits stay local and nothing is pushed.
  HEAD=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
  COUNT=$(git log --oneline "$START..HEAD" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  REMOTE=$(git branch -r --contains HEAD | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  if [ "$COUNT" = "0" ] || [ "$REMOTE" = "0" ]; then echo "C7 PASS head=$HEAD commits_since_start=$COUNT remote_branches_with_head=$REMOTE" >> "$C"; else echo "C7 FAIL head=$HEAD start=$START commits_since_start=$COUNT remote_branches_with_head=$REMOTE" >> "$C"; fi

  cat "$C"
  PASSES=$(grep -c '^C[1-7] PASS ' "$C")
  N="$PASSES" perl -0pi -e 's{^(\| BASE_REF \|.*\n)}{$1 . "| Parent criteria | Done | $ENV{N} PASS lines in scratch/closeout/criteria.txt |\n"}me unless /\| Parent criteria \|/' "$PKT/goal.md"
  echo "CRITERIA_LOGGED passes=$PASSES"
  ```

  node .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/repair-derived.cjs --folder "$PHASE" --apply
  node .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/repair-derived.cjs --folder "$PKT" --apply
  node .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/repair-derived.cjs --folder specs/system-speckit/041-write-recipe-fixes --apply
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PHASE" --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PKT" --recursive --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1
  bash $S/runtime/cli/rules/check-placeholders.sh "$PHASE"; echo "placeholder exit=$?"
  node .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-goal/scripts/check-goal.cjs "$PKT" | tail -1
  git status --porcelain > "$SC/baseline/status-end.txt"
  diff "$SC/baseline/status-start.txt" "$SC/baseline/status-end.txt" | grep '^>' | sed 's/^> //' > "$SC/baseline/status-delta.txt"
  cat "$SC/baseline/status-delta.txt"
  awk '{print $2}' "$SC/baseline/status-delta.txt" | rg -v '^(\.skilled/(skills/system-spec-kit/(benchmark/reports/|SKILL\.md$|runtime/data/trigger-index\.json$|runtime/cli/retrieval/fixtures/(corpus-manifest|generation-diagnostics|phrase-variants)\.json$)|changelog/system-spec-kit/v2\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.md$)|specs/system-speckit/(graph-metadata\.json$|041-write-recipe-fixes/spec\.md$|054-weak-model-spec-authoring/(spec\.md$|goal\.md$|changelog/|009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/)))' | wc -l | tr -d ' '
  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PKT/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[x\]'
  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PKT/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[ \]' || true
  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PHASE/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[x\]'
  ```

<!-- ANCHOR:directive -->
## 1. DURABLE DIRECTIVE

**Objective:** Measure the same frozen authoring task on the final tree, report the before and after for five metrics as a range, and close the packet with every generated artifact and every status row reconciled to that one observed state.

### Decisions

Frozen choices. Changing one is an amendment.

| ID | Decision |
|----|----------|
| D1 | The run brief is `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/brief-1.txt` byte for byte, the phase 1 T009 task at Level 3. No word changes on the final side. |
| D2 | M2 counts bare v2.2 hint occurrences plus v2.3 value token occurrences. The two patterns are disjoint and each unfilled instruction counts once. |
| D3 | Three final runs, serial, on cli-devin `swe-2-max`, compared with the 3 `swe-2-max` baseline runs. No run is dropped when it is bad. |
| D4 | The report folder is `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/benchmark/reports/$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline/`, dated by execution. |
| D5 | This phase is the only one allowed to edit the parent `spec.md`, the parent `goal.md` and the 041 phase map row for `006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination`. |
| D6 | A parent goal box is ticked only after its `C<n>` line reads PASS in `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt`. |
| D7 | Nothing is committed and nothing is pushed. Both stay with the operator. |

<!-- /ANCHOR:directive -->

| Run protocol on 2026-10-25 | The runs mirror the script that produced the SWE 2 Max baseline. Each dispatch uses the baseline brief-1.txt copied byte for byte (cmp), runs under a 2700 second watchdog on cli-devin swe-2-max with --permission-mode dangerous, and records its exit status. M5 is the dispatch end second minus the dispatch start second, as in the baseline, not the measurement time. M2 is the bare hint count (hints-l3.txt) plus the value token count. M4 is the status diff, and a change in the hash of git diff -- .skilled before and after a run also counts as an out-of-scope write. Each run's packet is copied to the scratchpad outside the worktree before it is removed. The session audit selects Devin sessions in the worktree between the start second and the end second plus 60, requires exactly one, and searches both tool_call_json and tool_call_update_json. A rate-limit or billing reply is not a run and is redone for the same run number. Extra per-run fields (exit status, bare and token split, audit) are in scratch/baseline/runs-meta.jsonl so baseline.json keeps the six keys per run. |

Run 3 still passed strict validation and took 1822 seconds, against 437 and 429 for the other two final runs and 317 to 384 for the baseline. The result is recorded as it is. <!-- ANCHOR:what-built -->
## What Was Built

The packet now ends with a measured before and after, and the measurement found one failure. Three final runs of the frozen Level 3 authoring task are recorded beside the SWE 2 Max baseline. Every final run passed strict validation, the final maximum of `leftover_hints` is 0 against a baseline maximum of 1, and no run wrote outside its track. Parent criterion 5 is FAIL on the frozen metric, because run 3 left 15 anchors in `tasks.md` where the baseline expects 21, so `missing_anchors` has a final maximum of 6 against 0.

The six anchors run 3 deleted are the optional Level 3 verification sections: `arch-verify`, `perf-verify`, `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `docs-verify` and `sign-off`. A diagnostic, chosen after the result and not a verdict, scores all six runs against the anchors the contract requires (`template-structure.js contract 3 tasks.md` lists 15 required and these 6 optional), and every run misses none. Three of the deletions went against the template's own fill notes, because the packet records ADR-001 (`plan.md` line 239 of run 3, kept as `scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/plan.txt`), states NFR-P01 (`spec.md` line 133 of run 3, kept as `scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/spec.txt`) and holds paired documents. The run's reply says "L3+ optional sections dropped (this is Level 3, not 3+)", so it read the `## L3+:` heading prefix as Level 3+ only. Run 3 also took 1822 seconds against 437 and 429, and the cause is unknown. The run log is `scratch/baseline/runs.jsonl`, the extras are in `runs-meta.jsonl` and the delta is `scratch/baseline/delta.json`.

### Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

The closeout also published the benchmark report with verdict FAIL, wrote the global changelog `v2.9.0.0` and bumped the version carrier, wrote the nested changelogs for phases 001 to 003 and this phase, regenerated the trigger index, confirmed the track roots and reconciled the parent phase map and goal log. `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt` holds one line for each parent criterion: six PASS lines and one FAIL line, `C5 FAIL`.

# 2026-10-25--model-eval--authoring-baseline

> system-spec-kit · model-eval · authoring-baseline · cli-devin swe-2-max

---

## 1. HEADLINE / OVERVIEW

**Verdict: FAIL**

Three baseline runs and three final runs of one frozen Level 3 authoring brief. Every final run passed strict validation, and leftover hints run from 0 to 1 on the baseline side and from 0 to 0 on the final side. The verdict is FAIL because the worst final run is worse than the worst baseline run on missing_anchors: a final maximum of 6 against 0. The verdict is PASS only when every final run passes strict validation and none is worse than the worst baseline run on leftover hints, missing anchors or out-of-scope writes.

C5 FAIL on the frozen metric, and the verdict stays FAIL. Run 3 deleted the six optional Level 3 verification sections in `tasks.md`: `arch-verify`, `perf-verify`, `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `docs-verify` and `sign-off`.

Diagnostic, chosen after the result and not a verdict. Scored against the anchors the Level 3 `tasks.md` contract requires (`template-structure.js contract 3 tasks.md`: 15 required, and these 6 optional), all six runs, three baseline and three final, miss 0 required anchors. The six deleted sections are the optional ones. Three of the deletions went against the template's own fill notes: the packet records ADR-001 (`plan.md` line 239 of run 3, kept as `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/plan.txt`), it states NFR-P01 (`spec.md` line 133 of run 3, kept as `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/spec.txt`), and it holds paired documents. The run's reply says 'L3+ optional sections dropped (this is Level 3, not 3+)', so it read the `## L3+:` heading prefix as meaning Level 3+ only. Leftover hints improved from a baseline maximum of 1 to 0. Run 3 took 1822 seconds against 437 and 429 for the other two, and the cause is unknown.

The operator chose to keep the frozen metric and the FAIL, and to add this diagnostic ("Option 3 + diagnostic").

---

## 2. AGGREGATE RESULTS

| Metric | Baseline min | Baseline max | Baseline mean | Final min | Final max | Final mean | Delta of means |
|--------|--------------|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|
| strict_passed | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 0 |
| leftover_hints | 0 | 1 | 0.7 | 0 | 0 | 0 | -0.7 |
| missing_anchors | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 6 | 2 | 2 |
| out_of_scope_writes | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| seconds | 317 | 384 | 342.3 | 429 | 1822 | 896 | 553.7 |

Each cell comes from three runs. The per-run rows are in `results.csv`.

---

## 3. METHODOLOGY

Fixture: one Level 3 packet for a `--quiet` flag on `recommend-level.sh`. The brief is `brief-1.txt` from the SWE 2 Max baseline, copied for each final run and compared with `cmp`, so no word changed. Each side is three serial runs by cli-devin `swe-2-max` on `--permission-mode dangerous` with a 2700 second watchdog. The measured documents are `spec.md`, `plan.md`, `tasks.md` and `acceptance-criteria.md`. The throwaway track is copied outside the worktree and then deleted after each measurement.

Metrics: `strict_passed` is the count of `RESULT: PASSED` lines from strict validation of the packet. `leftover_hints` counts bare v2.2 hint strings from the baseline list (121 patterns) plus v2.3 value tokens, so each unfilled instruction counts once whichever spelling the template used. `missing_anchors` sums the shortfall of each document against the baseline anchor counts (spec 7, plan 14, tasks 21, acceptance-criteria 3). `out_of_scope_writes` counts new lines in the git status after the run, outside the bench track and this phase folder. `seconds` is the dispatch end second minus the dispatch start second, the same clock the baseline used.

Each run's Devin session was found by working directory and start window, and its tool calls were searched in both the call and the update column for the baseline evidence folder, this phase's scratch folder and `/private/tmp`.

---

## 4. PER-CANDIDATE PROFILES

| Side | Tree | What the worker saw |
|---|---|---|
| baseline | Commit `ac2be31ec6`, before the packet changes | v2.2 templates, bare bracketed hints, the hand-written examples |
| final | Commit `a15bc0636e`, after phases 1 to 8 | v2.3 value tokens, section fill guidance, the authoring card, the guards |

---

## 5. PROCESS NOTES

Final runs 1 to 3 ran in order with nothing else touching the worktree. No attempt was discarded, and every dispatch exited with status 0. The audit found exactly one session per run and no tool call naming a forbidden path. The git diff of `.skilled` hashed the same before and after each run, and the status diff showed no write outside the bench track.

The three final runs took 437, 429, 1822 seconds. The three baseline runs took 326, 317, 384 seconds.

The report file is `README.md`, not the `benchmark-report.md` that the benchmark authoring skill names, because every existing folder under this reports root holds `README.md` and the index links to the folder.

---

## 6. FINDINGS

The load-bearing number is `missing_anchors`. Its final maximum is 6 against 0 on the baseline.
Run 3 wrote a `tasks.md` with 15 anchors where the baseline expects 21. Compared with run 1, it lacks `arch-verify`, `perf-verify`, `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `docs-verify`, `sign-off`. Strict validation still passed, so no validator rule catches those sections.

Scored against the contract-required anchors, all six runs miss 0 of 15 required `tasks.md` anchors. The six anchors run 3 deleted are optional in the contract.

Leftover hints fell: the final maximum is 0 and the baseline maximum is 1. Strict validation passed in all 3 final runs. No run wrote outside its track.

Time rose. Seconds per run ranged from 429 to 1822 on the final side against 317 to 384 on the baseline. Two final runs took about as long as each other (437 and 429), and run 3 took 1822.

---

## 7. CAVEATS

Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim. One run produced the whole regression, so the sample cannot say whether the omission comes from the template change or from ordinary run-to-run variation. The fixture is one small feature and four documents. The model is one model at one setting. A template change and a card change landed together, so this report cannot separate their effects.

The `spec.md`, `plan.md` and `tasks.md` of the three final packets, and the `tasks.md` of the three baseline packets, are kept as `.txt` copies under `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/bench-evidence/`. The rest of each final packet was copied to the conductor scratchpad outside the worktree and is not part of the repository. The raw run logs and audit files are in the source packet named in `source.md`.

---

## 8. RECOMMENDATIONS

- **Apply now.** Keep the already shipped template work as it stands, and do not read that as proof of a gain. Report the result as it is: C5 FAIL on the frozen metric, fewer leftover hints, and one run that deleted six optional Level 3 verification sections.
- **Validate first.** Make the `## L3+:` heading label unambiguous for Level 3 authors, then rerun this benchmark in a new phase with three or more runs on the same brief and see whether the omission recurs.
- **Future.** Decide whether strict validation should treat the optional Level 3 verification anchors as required when a packet records an ADR, a performance requirement or paired documents. That is a validator change and belongs to its own packet.

---

## 9. REPRODUCIBILITY

```bash
bash .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 3 --track zz-bench-hints --short-name recommend-level-quiet-flag "Add a quiet flag to recommend-level.sh"
rg -o --no-filename "\[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: [^]]*\]" specs/zz-bench-hints/001-recommend-level-quiet-flag/*.md | sort -u > hints.txt
```

The dispatch and the five metric commands are in `scratch/tools/run-final.sh` in the source packet. Run it as `run-final.sh N dispatch` and then `run-final.sh N measure`. The three final runs took about 45 minutes together.

## 1. DURABLE DIRECTIVE

**Objective:** A weak-model swarm authors valid, filled spec packets from the system-spec-kit templates, proved by a before and after measured on SWE 2 Max, with no change to the verdict of any existing packet.

### Decisions

Frozen choices. Changing one is an amendment.

| ID | Decision |
|----|----------|
| D1 | Phases 001 to 009, plus one write-recipe child under `041-write-recipe-fixes`. All logic lives in system-spec-kit, never sk-prompt. |
| D2 | The placeholder gate applies to new docs only, through the v2.3 template marker. No existing or external packet gets a new failure. |
| D3 | At most 3 parallel lanes, never two on one file. |
| D4 | Every phase closes on scripted gates plus one read-only review (cli-devin, swe-2-max). |
| D5 | Phase 008 runs the playbooks in a disposable sk-git worktree. |
| D6 | Every phase is Level 3 with its own goal, bound below. |
| D7 | The conductor is a Claude Code session. Execution and verification run on Sonnet 5.5 high subagents, plus cli-pi DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 max for single-change lanes. |
| D8 | The benchmark subject is cli-devin swe-2-max. Its 3 baseline runs rerun at the start commit, and phases 005 and 009 compare against them. |

# Feature Specification: Make system-spec-kit templates and authoring docs followable by a DeepSeek V4.1 Flash agent swarm

<!-- ANCHOR:metadata -->
## 1. METADATA

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Level** | 2 |
| **Priority** | P1 |
| **Status** | In Progress |
| **Created** | 2026-10-23 |

---

<!-- ANCHOR:problem -->
## 2. PROBLEM & PURPOSE

### Problem Statement
A weak-model worker (the benchmark subject is cli-devin `swe-2-max`, and single-change swarm lanes run on DeepSeek V4.1 Flash) that authors a spec packet from the system-spec-kit templates leaves scaffold hints in place and still passes `validate.sh --strict`. A fresh Level 2 scaffold holds 97 bracketed hint occurrences and validates clean. The hand-written examples the worker imitates carry leaked description text and stale lines. The authoring guidance is a 1,200-line template guide that a weak model cannot follow in one pass. Together these let a swarm produce packets that look finished and are not.

### Purpose
Make the templates, the examples, the validator and the authoring docs good enough that a weak-model swarm, measured on cli-devin `swe-2-max`, authors a valid, filled packet without a frontier model correcting it. Phase 001 measures the starting point and the last phase measures the result, so the claim rests on numbers.

> **Phase-parent note:** This spec.md is the ONLY authored document at the parent level. All detailed planning, task breakdowns, checklists, and decisions live in the child phase folders listed in the Phase Documentation Map below. This keeps the parent from drifting stale as phases execute and pivot.
<!-- /ANCHOR:problem -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:scope -->
## 3. SCOPE

### In Scope
- Repair the known template, example and reference defects, found by a census and fixed by exact text swap.
- Gate unfilled placeholders on new docs only, through a template version marker.
- Add a one-line fill instruction with a good and a weak example to each core template section.
- Add a short authoring card and route the skill router to it.
- Check and update the speckit and doctor commands and the doc-authoring agents such as markdown.
- Align the playbook, catalog and READMEs of system-spec-kit, then run the playbooks in a disposable worktree.
- Measure the authoring baseline before any change and again at the end.
- One write-recipe fix, tracked as a child of the earlier write-recipe series.

### Out of Scope
- Any change to `sk-prompt`. All logic lives in system-spec-kit.
- Any change that alters the verdict for an existing packet, because external-repo users would break on `/doctor:update`.
- Measuring models other than cli-devin swe-2-max for the benchmark.
- Pushing. Each phase ends in one local commit on the worktree branch and nothing is pushed.

### Files to Change

| File Path | Change Type | Phase | Description |
|-----------|-------------|-------|-------------|
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/templates/examples/**` | Modify | 002, 004 | Example descriptions, stale lines, skeleton alignment |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/references/**` | Modify | 002, 005, 006 | Style guide, checklist, template guide, new card |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/templates/core/*.tmpl` | Modify | 003, 004, 005 | Token form, v2.3 marker, fill guidance, task `Verify:` slot |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/rules/check-placeholders.sh` and its TypeScript mirror | Modify | 003 | Versioned placeholder gate |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/SKILL.md`, `leaf-manifest.json`, `leaf-aliases.json` | Modify | 005 | Router loads the card |
| `.skilled/commands/speckit/`, `.skilled/commands/doctor/` speckit entries, `.skilled/agents/markdown.md` and other doc-authoring agents | Modify | 006 | Commands and agents match the new templates, gate and card |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/` guard scripts | Create | 004, 005 | Example drift guard and card sync guard |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/manual-testing-playbook/`, `feature-catalog/`, READMEs | Modify | 007 | Scenarios and docs for the new behavior |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/benchmark/reports/` | Create | 008, 009 | Playbook run report and benchmark report |
| `specs/system-speckit/041-write-recipe-fixes/006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination/` | Create | 041-006 | Write-recipe move destination fix |

### Launch preconditions

The operator meets all four before the first phase runs. The conductor records each as met in `goal.md` section 4 and stops when one is not.

1. **Workspace.** The operator chooses A) a git worktree or B) the current branch, following `sk-git`. The conductor never chooses. The chosen path becomes `WORKTREE` in `goal.md` section 4.
2. **Provider login.** `devin auth status` reports a Devin login. A rate limit or refusal on cli-devin stops the run.
3. **Lane authorization.** At most 3 parallel lanes and never two on one file. The operator has already given this.
4. **Conductor placement.** The conductor is a Claude Code session. Execution and verification run on Sonnet 5.5 high subagents, plus cli-pi DeepSeek V4.1 Flash for single-change lanes, and every review runs on cli-devin `swe-2-max`, except the phase 009 closeout review, which the operator moved to cli-codex `gpt-6-luna` at max effort on the fast tier (approved 2026-10-25, recorded in the phase 009 `goal.md` section 4). The benchmark subject is cli-devin `swe-2-max`.
<!-- /ANCHOR:scope -->

<!-- ANCHOR:phase-map -->
## PHASE DOCUMENTATION MAP

> This spec uses phased decomposition. Each phase is an independently executable child spec folder. All implementation details (plan, tasks, checklist, decisions, continuity) live inside the phase children.

| Phase | Folder | Focus | Status |
|-------|--------|-------|--------|
| 1 | 001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/ | Census every template, example and reference defect into a verified ledger and measure the authoring baseline | Complete |
| 2 | 002-template-and-example-defect-repair/ | Apply the ledger rows owned by this phase with a tested exact-match applier | Complete |
| 3 | 003-versioned-placeholder-gate/ | Make unfilled `[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: ...]` tokens a Draft warning and a later error, for v2.3 docs only | Complete |
| 4 | 004-template-section-fill-guidance/ | Add a fill instruction and a good and weak example to each core section, and guard example drift | Complete |
| 5 | 005-weak-model-authoring-card/ | Write the authoring card, route the skill to it and guard the sync | Complete |
| 6 | 006-speckit-command-and-agent-alignment/ | Check and update the speckit and doctor commands and the doc-authoring agents such as markdown for the new templates, gate and card | Complete |
| 7 | 007-playbook-catalog-readme-alignment/ | Update the playbook, catalog and READMEs for the new behavior | Complete |
| 8 | 008-playbook-run-in-worktree/ | Run every new or changed playbook scenario in a disposable worktree and report PASS or FAIL | Complete |
| 9 | 009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/ | Rerun the baseline, report the delta per metric and close the packet | In Progress |

### Phase Transition Rules

- Each phase MUST pass `validate.sh` independently before the next phase begins
- Parent spec tracks aggregate progress via this map
- Use `/spec_kit:resume [parent-folder]/[NNN-phase]/` to resume a specific phase
- Run `validate.sh --recursive` on parent to validate all phases as integrated unit

### Phase Handoff Criteria

| From | To | Criteria | Verification |
|------|-----|----------|--------------|
| 001-template-drift-census-and-baseline | 002-template-and-example-defect-repair | `ledger.jsonl` exists, every row reproduces and the baseline holds three runs | `jq -c . ledger.jsonl` exits 0 and `baseline.json` holds 3 runs |
| 002-template-and-example-defect-repair | 003-versioned-placeholder-gate | Every owner-002 row no longer reproduces and the suite result matches the phase 001 start | Row evidence commands print nothing and the suite summary lines match `suite-start.txt` |
| 003-versioned-placeholder-gate | 004-template-section-fill-guidance | Core hints are tokens, the marker is v2.3 and recursive strict counts are unchanged | The failing-first test passes and the before and after counts match |
| 004-template-section-fill-guidance | 005-weak-model-authoring-card | Every core section has guidance, goldens pass and the drift guard passes | The golden test and the drift guard script exit 0 |
| 005-weak-model-authoring-card | 006-speckit-command-and-agent-alignment | The card exists, the router loads it and the sync guard passes | The sync guard script exits 0 and the workflow invariance test passes |
| 006-speckit-command-and-agent-alignment | 007-playbook-catalog-readme-alignment | Every speckit and doctor command and every doc-authoring agent names the card, the v2.3 token and the guard scripts where it describes authoring | The phase 006 audit table has no open row |
| 007-playbook-catalog-readme-alignment | 008-playbook-run-in-worktree | Every new scenario file exists and the playbook index lists it | The playbook index lists each scenario id |
| 008-playbook-run-in-worktree | 009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout | Every scenario has a recorded PASS or an escalated FAIL | The report folder holds a row for each scenario |

# Acceptance Criteria: Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

<!-- HVR_REFERENCE: .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-with-human-voice/references/hvr-rules.md -->

> This document decides whether the packet may close. A packet is closeable when
> every row below is `Met`, `Waived` or `Superseded`. A `Waived` or `Superseded`
> row MUST name an ADR that exists in `decision-record.md`.

---

**Packet:** system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout
**Level:** 3
**Status:** In Progress
**Date:** 2026-10-23

### Status values

| Value | Meaning |
|-------|---------|
| `Met` | Verified. The Verification cell names evidence that was actually observed. |
| `Unmet` | Not yet satisfied. Blocks closure. |
| `Waived` | Deliberately not pursued. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |
| `Superseded` | Replaced by a different criterion or decision. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |

### Waiver cell

Write `-` when the row is `Met` or `Unmet`. Write `ADR-NNN` when the row is
`Waived` or `Superseded`, naming a decision record that exists in
`decision-record.md`. A waiver naming an ADR that is not there fails validation:
the point of a waiver is that someone recorded the reasoning, so an unbacked
waiver is treated as an unmet criterion rather than as a pass.
<!-- /ANCHOR:criteria -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:closure -->
## 3. CLOSURE STATEMENT

**Closeable:** No

Twelve of the fifteen rows are Met. AC-010 stays Unmet because the parent phase map holds eight Complete rows and phase 9 reads In Progress, AC-011 stays Unmet because `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt` holds six PASS lines and the C5 FAIL line, and AC-015 stays Unmet because the phase cannot read Complete while criterion 5 is FAIL. The packet closes only after the follow-up benchmark rerun passes criterion 5.
<!-- /ANCHOR:closure -->

# Goal: Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

<!-- HVR_REFERENCE: .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-with-human-voice/references/hvr-rules.md -->
<!-- GOAL_AUTHORING: .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-goal/SKILL.md -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:directive -->
## 1. DURABLE DIRECTIVE

**Objective:** Measure the same frozen authoring task on the final tree, report the before and after for five metrics as a range, and close the packet with every generated artifact and every status row reconciled to that one observed state.

### Decisions

Frozen choices. Changing one is an amendment.

| ID | Decision |
|----|----------|
| D1 | The run brief is `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/brief-1.txt` byte for byte, the phase 1 T009 task at Level 3. No word changes on the final side. |
| D2 | M2 counts bare v2.2 hint occurrences plus v2.3 value token occurrences. The two patterns are disjoint and each unfilled instruction counts once. |
| D3 | Three final runs, serial, on cli-devin `swe-2-max`, compared with the 3 `swe-2-max` baseline runs. No run is dropped when it is bad. |
| D4 | The report folder is `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/benchmark/reports/$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline/`, dated by execution. |
| D5 | This phase is the only one allowed to edit the parent `spec.md`, the parent `goal.md` and the 041 phase map row for `006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination`. |
| D6 | A parent goal box is ticked only after its `C<n>` line reads PASS in `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt`. |
| D7 | Nothing is committed and nothing is pushed. Both stay with the operator. |

<!-- /ANCHOR:directive -->

---


<!-- ANCHOR:completion -->
## 3. COMPLETION CRITERIA

- [x] `jq '.runs | length' scratch/baseline/baseline.json` prints `3` and each run holds the five metrics `strict_passed`, `leftover_hints`, `missing_anchors`, `out_of_scope_writes` and `seconds`
- [ ] `jq '.metrics | length' scratch/baseline/delta.json` prints `5`, every metric carries a minimum and a maximum for both sides, every final run passes strict validation, and the final maximum of `leftover_hints`, `missing_anchors` and `out_of_scope_writes` is at most the baseline maximum
- [x] The benchmark report folder holds `README.md` with ten numbered sections, `results.csv` with six runs, `source.md` that names this packet, and one new row in the reports index
- [x] `bash .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring --recursive --strict` prints `RESULT: PASSED`
- [ ] `grep -c '^C[1-7] PASS ' scratch/closeout/criteria.txt` prints `7` and the parent goal completion anchor holds seven `[x]` rows
- [x] `git branch -r --contains HEAD | wc -l` prints `0` whenever `git log --oneline <start>..HEAD` prints any line, so swarm commits stay local and nothing was pushed
<!-- /ANCHOR:completion -->

| Run protocol on 2026-10-25 | The runs mirror the script that produced the SWE 2 Max baseline. Each dispatch uses the baseline brief-1.txt copied byte for byte (cmp), runs under a 2700 second watchdog on cli-devin swe-2-max with --permission-mode dangerous, and records its exit status. M5 is the dispatch end second minus the dispatch start second, as in the baseline, not the measurement time. M2 is the bare hint count (hints-l3.txt) plus the value token count. M4 is the status diff, and a change in the hash of git diff -- .skilled before and after a run also counts as an out-of-scope write. Each run's packet is copied to the scratchpad outside the worktree before it is removed. The session audit selects Devin sessions in the worktree between the start second and the end second plus 60, requires exactly one, and searches both tool_call_json and tool_call_update_json. A rate-limit or billing reply is not a run and is redone for the same run number. Extra per-run fields (exit status, bare and token split, audit) are in scratch/baseline/runs-meta.jsonl so baseline.json keeps the six keys per run. |

<!-- ANCHOR:what-built -->
## What Was Built

The packet now ends with a measured before and after, and the measurement found one failure. Three final runs of the frozen Level 3 authoring task are recorded beside the SWE 2 Max baseline. Every final run passed strict validation, the final maximum of `leftover_hints` is 0 against a baseline maximum of 1, and no run wrote outside its track. Parent criterion 5 is FAIL on the frozen metric, because run 3 left 15 anchors in `tasks.md` where the baseline expects 21, so `missing_anchors` has a final maximum of 6 against 0.

The six anchors run 3 deleted are the optional Level 3 verification sections: `arch-verify`, `perf-verify`, `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `docs-verify` and `sign-off`. A diagnostic, chosen after the result and not a verdict, scores all six runs against the anchors the contract requires (`template-structure.js contract 3 tasks.md` lists 15 required and these 6 optional), and every run misses none. Three of the deletions went against the template's own fill notes, because the packet records ADR-001 (`plan.md` line 239 of run 3, kept as `scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/plan.txt`), states NFR-P01 (`spec.md` line 133 of run 3, kept as `scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/spec.txt`) and holds paired documents. The run's reply says "L3+ optional sections dropped (this is Level 3, not 3+)", so it read the `## L3+:` heading prefix as Level 3+ only. Run 3 also took 1822 seconds against 437 and 429, and the cause is unknown. The run log is `scratch/baseline/runs.jsonl`, the extras are in `runs-meta.jsonl` and the delta is `scratch/baseline/delta.json`.

### Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

The closeout also published the benchmark report with verdict FAIL, wrote the global changelog `v2.9.0.0` and bumped the version carrier, wrote the nested changelogs for phases 001 to 003 and this phase, regenerated the trigger index, confirmed the track roots and reconciled the parent phase map and goal log. `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt` holds one line for each parent criterion: six PASS lines and one FAIL line, `C5 FAIL`.

<!-- ANCHOR:how-delivered -->
## How It Was Delivered

One Claude Code worker ran the phase and spawned no subagent. It ran three serial cli-devin swe-2-max dispatches of the frozen Level 3 brief and audited each run's Devin session. It measured each run with the five commands recorded in `scratch/tools/run-final.sh`, wrote every number in the report from `delta.json` and the run files, and proved each parent criterion before ticking any box. The census for criterion 3 was rerun over the frozen folder list, which excludes this packet. One read-only frontier review on cli-codex gpt-6-luna read the closeout diff, and each finding is recorded in `goal.md` section 4. The operator moved the review from the cli-devin swe-2-max that parent D4 names.

### Operator handoff

- This phase made one local commit on the worktree branch. Nothing was pushed.
- The follow-up is open: make the `## L3+:` heading label unambiguous for Level 3 authors, then rerun the benchmark in a new phase.
- The parent `spec.md` and this phase are In Progress, not Complete, until that rerun passes criterion 5.
- No generated artifact needs a hand edit before review.

<!-- ANCHOR:decisions -->
## Key Decisions

| Decision | Why |
|----------|-----|
| Keep the frozen metric and the FAIL, and add a labelled diagnostic | The operator chose "Option 3 + diagnostic". Changing the metric after the result would hide the finding |
| Count bare hints plus value tokens for M2 | The two patterns are disjoint, so each unfilled instruction counts once on both sides |
| Use Level 3 for the final runs | The SWE 2 Max baseline is Level 3 |
| Prove parent criterion 3 on the frozen folder list | A live walk would compare different populations |
| Do not mark the phase or the parent Complete | The validator reads a Complete status with an unmet acceptance row as a failed closure |

<!-- ANCHOR:limitations -->
## Known Limitations

1. **Parent criterion 5 is FAIL.** `missing_anchors` has a final maximum of 6 against a baseline maximum of 0, and the phase and the parent stay In Progress.
2. **Open follow-up.** Make the `## L3+:` heading label unambiguous for Level 3 authors, then rerun the benchmark in a new phase.
3. **Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim.** One run produced the whole failure, so the sample cannot separate the template change from run-to-run variation.
4. **The fixture is one small feature and four documents.** A different feature shape or a Level 1 packet is not measured.
5. **Acceptance rows AC-010, AC-011 and AC-015 are Unmet.** They need nine Complete map rows, seven PASS lines and a Complete status, which criterion 5 prevents.
<!-- /ANCHOR:limitations -->

# Tasks: Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

<!-- ANCHOR:notation -->
## Task Notation

| Prefix | Meaning |
|--------|---------|
| `[ ]` | Pending |
| `[x]` | Completed |
| `[P]` | Parallelizable |
| `[B]` | Blocked |

**Task Format**: `T### [P?] Description (file path)`, then `Verify:` with the command whose output proves the task.

Shell variables used below, set once by the conductor:
- `WORKTREE`: the build worktree path recorded in `../goal.md` section 4.
- `PKT`: `$WORKTREE/specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring`
- `PHASE`: `$PKT/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout`
- `SC`: `$PHASE/scratch`
- `S`: `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit`
- `P1`: `$PKT/001-template-drift-census-and-baseline`
- `BL`: `$P1/scratch/baseline-swe-l3`, the Level 3 cli-devin swe-2-max baseline.
- `P3`: `$PKT/003-versioned-placeholder-gate`
- `START`: the commit hash in `$SC/baseline/start-commit.txt`, recorded by T003.

Every command runs from `$WORKTREE` unless the task says otherwise. Numbers printed in a Verify line that depend on the run are written as a rule, not as a fixed value. The baseline side is read from `$BL`: `baseline.json` for the three runs, `brief-1.txt` for the frozen brief, `hints-l3.txt` for the bare hint list and `anchors-l3.txt` for the required anchors.
<!-- /ANCHOR:notation -->

- [x] T002 Check that every prior phase and the write-recipe child are closed (no file). Run the block below. Verify: the first line reads `8 RESULT: PASSED`, the second reads `RESULT: PASSED`, then `BASELINE_OK`, `CENSUS_OK`, `DRIFT_GUARD_OK`, `CARD_GUARD_OK`, `NESTED_OK` and a final `RESULT: PASSED`. When one line is missing or a child fails, write the missing path or the failing child in `goal.md` section 4 and stop the phase. This phase does not edit another phase folder.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  for d in $PKT/00[1-8]-*/; do bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$d" --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1; done | sort | uniq -c | sed 's/^ *//'
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh specs/system-speckit/041-write-recipe-fixes/006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1

  test -s "$S/runtime/cli/dist/spec-folder/nested-changelog.js" && echo NESTED_OK
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PKT" --recursive --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1
  ```

Verify: `grep -cE 'Phase start commit \| Done \| [0-9a-f]{40}' "$PHASE/goal.md"` prints `1`, `tail -1 "$SC/baseline/suite-final.txt"` prints `exit=0`, and `wc -l < "$SC/baseline/status-start.txt"` prints a number.   ```
